## Live Coverage Gap Audit (2026-06-16)

This audit is intentionally stricter than the progress snapshot above.

Meaning:
- `verified` means the area has enough evidence to be accepted for the current QA baseline.
- `live gap` means at least one sub-scenario was not proven by a real end-to-end run on a device with production-like services.
- A live gap is not automatically an app bug. It is a testing debt item to schedule before calling the mobile app fully live-exhausted.

### Gap Policy

- Do not reopen a verified bug only because a live gap exists.
- Create a new `MB-*` bug only when the live gap run reproduces product failure.
- Mark a gap closed by adding date, device/build, exact steps, and result under the corresponding area section.
- Prefer release builds for `P0/P1` live gaps unless the scenario is explicitly dev-runtime-only.

## Purpose
This document defines all testing areas for the mobile app (`apps/mobile`) so testing is not limited to one feature at a time.

Use it as:
- a master map of what should be tested,
- an execution queue for focused test sessions,
- an index to attach discovered bugs in `docs/MOBILE_BUG_REGISTRY.md`.

## Test execution model
1. User picks area(s) from this catalog.
2. Codex runs manual + technical checks.
3. Codex logs issues into `docs/MOBILE_BUG_REGISTRY.md`.
4. Codex reports pass/fail summary and next-risk areas.

## Priority levels
- `P0`: critical user path or auth/session blocker.
- `P1`: core product workflow used frequently.
- `P2`: secondary workflow, quality and UX stability.
- `P3`: low-risk, cosmetic, or rare scenario.

## A. Platform and runtime foundation

### A1. App launch and bootstrapping (`P0`)
- cold start / warm start behavior
- splash-to-first-screen transition
- crash-free startup and recovery after background kill

### A2. Environment and config wiring (`P0`)
- required `EXPO_PUBLIC_*` variables are present
- expected API base URL behavior
- fallback behavior for missing/invalid config

LG-005 completion (2026-06-17):
- PASS: built and installed a controlled x86_64 release APK with `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL=ht!tp://bad` and `EXPO_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=staging`.
- FAIL before fix: the installed app crashed during startup before `AppRegistry.registerComponent`, with `Invalid API URL config` followed by `main has not been registered` / `FATAL EXCEPTION` in logcat.
- FIX: moved mobile API URL resolution out of module scope in the shared mobile API client and WhatsApp auth screen so invalid config is thrown inside the React tree and caught by `ErrorBoundary`.
- PASS after fix: rebuilt the malformed APK and verified the installed app renders a user-visible ErrorBoundary screen containing `Invalid API URL config`, `EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL`, and `valid http(s) URL`.
- PASS: no `FATAL EXCEPTION` or `main has not been registered` startup crash was observed after the fix.
- PASS: rebuilt and reinstalled a valid-config x86_64 release APK; the emulator returned to the normal Sign-in screen.
- conclusion: `LG-005` is closed for the current Android emulator release baseline; product bug tracked as `MB-061`.
- evidence:
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_bad_api_url_launch.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_bad_api_url_logcat.txt`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_bad_api_url_fixed_launch.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_bad_api_url_fixed_launch.xml`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_bad_api_url_fixed_logcat.txt`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_valid_after_restore.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg005_valid_after_restore.xml`

### A3. Deep links and app scheme (`P0`)
- `kezek://` deep links
- auth callback deep links
- links from external browser/app back into mobile app

### A4. Network/offline baseline (`P1`)
- no-network startup
- network loss during active flows
- recovery after reconnect

LG-006 live run (2026-06-17):
- method:
  - `127.0.0.1:9` proxy blackhole was useful for plain mobile API fetch checks, but was not strict enough for all Supabase calls on this emulator.
  - The accepted true-network-loss method for this pass was `adb shell svc wifi disable` + `adb shell svc data disable`, restored with the matching `enable` commands.
- PASS: authenticated cold start with true network loss stayed crash-free, kept the user in Main navigation, and showed a clear Home offline/error state.
- PASS: Profile save under true network loss showed a user-visible offline toast: `Нет подключения к интернету. Проверьте соединение и попробуйте снова.`
- PASS: after network restore, Profile save recovered and showed success.
- PASS: Home manual recovery via pull-to-refresh restored the business list after reconnect.
- FAIL before fix: Home did not automatically retry the business list after reconnect despite copy saying it would update automatically; tracked and fixed as `MB-062`.
- PARTIAL: Booking wizard branch selection survived network loss, but the available test branch returned `Нет доступных услуг` even after reconnect, so final booking creation under true network loss was not proven in this pass.
- PARTIAL: Shift sync live verification was not reachable from the current authenticated session; the `Бизнес` tab exposed only the web-cabinet entry, not the native Shift workspace.
- automated verification after fix:
  - PASS: `corepack pnpm -C apps/mobile test --runInBand src/__tests__/screens/HomeScreen.test.tsx` (`10/10` tests).
  - PASS: `corepack pnpm -C apps/mobile typecheck`.
- follow-up:
  - rebuild/reinstall APK and rerun Home reconnect live check for `MB-062`;
  - rerun Shift true-network-loss sync on a native staff session.
- evidence:
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_auth_session_start_svc_offline.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_auth_session_recovered_online.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_home_recovered_pull_refresh.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_profile_save_svc_offline_1s.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_profile_save_svc_recovered_1s.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_booking_branch_svc_offline.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_booking_next_svc_offline.png`
  - `apps/mobile/lg006_business_tab_online2.png`

## C. Authentication and session

### C1. Sign-in UI/UX (`P0`)

- state cards/messages visibility and spacing

### C2. Google sign-in flow (`P0`)
- happy path login
- cancel path
- callback handling and session establishment
- retry/idempotency behavior

Live status (2026-06-01):
- cancel path: verified (Back from external Google auth returned to sign-in card).
- retry/idempotency: verified (repeat tap reopens auth flow consistently).
- callback/session establishment: verified (manual Google completion returned to app session).
- happy path login: verified (user completed real Google auth; app opened authenticated `Main`).

### C3. Telegram sign-in flow (`P0`)
- start flow and external handoff
- pending status behavior
- return-from-external-app behavior
- timeout/cancel/failure handling

Live status (2026-06-01):

LG-003 completion (2026-06-17):
- PASS: Android emulator `emulator-5554` opened a clean unauthenticated Sign-in screen after app data reset.
- PASS: the WhatsApp sign-in CTA navigated to the WhatsApp phone-entry screen.
- PASS: a real WhatsApp OTP request was sent to the configured test number and the OTP screen displayed the sent-code state.
- PASS: submitting invalid code `000000` did not create a session and kept the user on the OTP screen.
- PASS: an expired OTP produced a clear user-visible error and the resend action successfully requested a fresh code with cooldown.
- PASS: submitting the fresh valid OTP established an authenticated session and reset routing into the authenticated Home/Cabinet tab shell.
- PASS: force-stop/relaunch restored the authenticated Home state (`home-search-input` present), with no return to the OTP or Sign-in screen.
- conclusion: `LG-003` is closed for the current Android emulator release baseline.

### F2. Shift quick workspace (`P0`)
- open/close shift
- add/edit shift clients/items
- offline queue visibility and sync/retry behavior
- status: `verified`
- live result (2026-06-03, post-deploy):
  - PASS: `GET /api/staff/finance` succeeds on production with the mobile Bearer staff session.
  - PASS: staff user can open Staff tab -> My shift; ShiftQuick renders active/closed shift states with readable Russian text.
  - PASS: open shift flow works; `POST /api/staff/shift/open` returns success and the UI switches to active shift state.
  - PASS: manual client/item add works; `POST /api/staff/shift/items` returns success and persisted metrics show turnover `500`, master `300`, salon `300`, clients `1`.
  - PASS: close shift flow works; confirmation dialog appears, `POST /api/staff/shift/close` returns success, and the UI switches to closed shift state while preserving the client item.
  - PASS: manual shift client/item cards expose edit while the shift is open.
  - PASS: edit mode reuses the client form, validates required fields, saves through `/api/staff/shift/items`, and refreshes the visible card.

### G3. Nearby/discovery interactions (`P2`)
- location-related UI handling
- fallback behavior if location unavailable
- status: `verified`
- implementation update (2026-06-17):
  - Added native foreground-location request flow with `expo-location`.
  - Home calls `/api/branches/nearby` with `lat`, `lon`, `limit=5`, and `radiusKm=20`.
  - Nearby branch results show business, branch, address, distance, and open the booking flow through the business slug.
  - Permission denied, location unavailable, empty result, loading, and request-error states now have explicit user-visible copy.
- automated verification (2026-06-17):
  - PASS: `HomeScreen` renders the nearby discovery entry point.
  - PASS: granted location permission calls `/api/branches/nearby` and renders returned distance-ranked branch rows.
  - PASS: denied location permission renders a clear fallback state.
  - PASS: `corepack pnpm -C apps/mobile test --runInBand src/__tests__/screens/HomeScreen.test.tsx` (`12/12`).
  - PASS: `corepack pnpm -C apps/mobile typecheck`.
- physical-device live verification (2026-06-17, Android device `8231be4e2ca4`, ARM64 debug build + Metro):
  - PASS: rebuilt and installed ARM64 debug APK with native `expo-location`.
  - PASS: Home loaded authenticated state on the physical device and rendered the `Ближайшие филиалы` card with `Найти рядом`.
  - PASS: tapping `Найти рядом` opened the real Android foreground-location permission dialog with Kezek app copy.
  - PASS: allowing foreground location returned real device coordinates and called `/api/branches/nearby?lat=40.524387&lon=72.7981101&limit=5&radiusKm=20`.
  - PASS: Home rendered distance-ranked nearby branches including `Manly` at `1.4 км`, `Образ` at `2.1 км`, and `Low Fade`.
  - PASS: tapping the `Manly` nearby branch opened the booking flow at `Шаг 1 из 6 / Филиал` with `Manly Фрунзенская` selected and `Дальше` enabled.
  - PASS: emulator fallback run plus automated tests cover denied permission, unavailable location, current-position timeout, and last-known-position fallback.
  - LIMIT: a clean physical deny-dialog replay was not completed because the dev-client returned to the server picker after permission revocation; denied behavior remains covered by automated tests and the fallback UI contract.
  - conclusion: nearby list discovery is verified for the current Android QA baseline.
  - evidence:
    - `apps/mobile/lg007_physical_permission.xml`
    - `apps/mobile/lg007_physical_after_allow.png`
    - `apps/mobile/lg007_physical_after_allow.xml`

- map parity implementation and live verification (2026-06-17, physical Android device `8231be4e2ca4`):

  - PASS: the native `Map` shell embeds web `/map?mobile=1`, preserving the web Yandex map experience with category filtering, nearest CTA, branch list, and visible branch markers.
  - PASS: tapping a map booking link opens the native booking flow at `Шаг 1 из 6 / Филиал` instead of leaving the user inside the embedded web booking page.
  - PASS: targeted map/linking/Home tests and mobile typecheck pass.
  - Tracked bug `MB-064` is verified.
  - evidence:
    - `apps/mobile/mb064_usb_map.png`
    - `apps/mobile/mb064_usb_map.xml`
    - `apps/mobile/mb064_usb_booking_intercept_fixed.png`
    - `apps/mobile/mb064_usb_booking_intercept_fixed.xml`

### I1. Typography and spacing consistency (`P1`)
- hierarchy correctness on small/large mobile widths
- no clipped text in RU localization
- status: `verified`
- live verification (2026-06-08):
  - PASS: Home, Cabinet, and owner Dashboard checked on Android at approximately `360dp` and `411dp`.
  - PASS: auth typography at the same width class remains covered by the completed C1 visual session.
  - PASS: page titles, section titles, captions, search text, cards, CTA labels, and bottom-tab labels remain readable in Russian.
  - PASS: scroll content retains enough bottom space to move actions above the floating tab bar.
  - PASS after fix: Cabinet email, hero copy, status pill, and metric labels no longer wrap character-by-character or clip.
- responsive rules added:
  - Cabinet header stacks below or at `380dp`.
  - Cabinet overview status moves below the copy below or at `380dp`.
  - Cabinet metrics use a `2 + 1` grid below or at `420dp`.
- automated verification:
  - PASS: Home/Cabinet/Dashboard targeted suites (`19/19`).
  - PASS: Cabinet regression suite (`6/6`).
  - PASS: `uiTextThemeSmoke` (`2/2`) and mobile typecheck.

## J. Error handling and resilience

### J1. API error handling (`P0`)
- user-visible errors are understandable
- no silent failures for critical actions
- status: `verified`
- source audit:
  - API, Supabase, auth, booking, profile, and shift mutation error paths were reviewed.
  - Technical messages (`Network request failed`, `HTTP 500`, JWT/PostgREST details, English exceptions) are now mapped to safe Russian copy.
  - Safe localized validation messages can still pass through without losing useful context.
  - Profile update/sign-out, booking create/cancel, OTP, Telegram, WhatsApp, and shift actions use the shared mapping policy.
  - Shift offline operations no longer report success if SecureStore fails to persist the queue.
- live verification (Android `emulator-5554`, 2026-06-09):
  - Disabled Wi-Fi/mobile data and enabled airplane mode.
  - Triggered a real read request from Home search.
  - PASS: UI showed ### J2. Retry and fallback logic (`P1`)
- transient network failures
- action retries and safe idempotency

Status: `verified` (2026-06-09)

- source audit:
  - query retries are limited to transient network, timeout, `408`, `425`, `429`, and `5xx` failures;
  - permanent client/domain errors are not retried;
  - WhatsApp send/verify retries reuse a stable idempotency key for the same logical request;
  - shift offline queue compacts duplicate adjacent operations and obsolete consecutive item snapshots.
- live verification:
  - disabled emulator Wi-Fi/data and enabled airplane mode;
  - triggered a fresh Home search and confirmed the user-visible error state;
  - restored connectivity and confirmed `kezek.kg` was reachable;
  - retried with `Low Fade`, confirmed live results loaded, and confirmed the error state disappeared.
- LG-006 update (2026-06-17):
  - true network loss was repeated with `adb shell svc wifi disable` and `adb shell svc data disable`;
  - Home stayed crash-free and showed offline/error copy during authenticated cold start;
  - Profile save recovered correctly after reconnect;
  - Home manual pull-to-refresh recovered the list after reconnect;
  - found and fixed `MB-062`: Home did not auto-retry the business list when network status returned online.
- automated verification:
  - retry classification and retry-count contract;
  - stable idempotency-key reuse and input-change rotation;
  - WhatsApp network-failure retry reuses the same request key;
  - shift queue deduplication and operation-boundary preservation;
  - PASS: full mobile suite (`34/34` suites, `162/162` tests), typecheck, and `git diff --check`.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-045`

### J3. App state transitions (`P1`)
- foreground/background
- interrupted auth/booking flows

Status: `verified` (2026-06-09)

- live verification:
  - entered `j3state` on Home, sent the app to the Android launcher, and reopened it;
  - confirmed Home remained mounted and preserved the search input;
  - opened `kezek://booking/low-fade`, selected a service on step `2/6`, interrupted the flow with the system Home action, and reopened the app;
  - confirmed the booking flow returned to step `2/6`, retained the selected service, and kept the Next action enabled;
  - authenticated session remained active across both foreground/background transitions.
- auth interruption coverage:
  - root session is synchronized whenever the app becomes active;
  - pending Google, Telegram, and WhatsApp attempts already persist recovery metadata and are covered by their auth suites and prior live external-app return sessions.
- stale-data recovery:
  - React Query focus now follows React Native `AppState`;
  - active stale queries refetch after foreground;
  - booking slot queries are immediately stale so availability is refreshed after returning to the time-selection flow.
- automated verification:
  - AppState-to-query-focus mapping and subscription cleanup;
  - actual stale-query refetch after background -> active;
  - auth session synchronization on app resume;
  - booking navigation regression suite;
  - targeted PASS: `3/3` suites, `18/18` tests;
  - full PASS: `35/35` suites, `166/166` tests, typecheck, and `git diff --check`.
- scope note:
  - this area covers normal foreground/background interruptions; restoring an unfinished booking after OS process death would require a separate persisted booking-draft feature.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-046`

## K. Accessibility and usability

### K1. Touch targets and hit areas (`P1`)
- critical actions meet practical tap sizes

Status: `verified` (2026-06-09)

- implementation:
  - introduced shared `MIN_TOUCH_TARGET = 44`;
  - booking close, completed-step navigation dots, Home search clear, feedback close, filters, chips, slots, and shift period controls use the shared minimum;
  - compact icon actions retain their visual icon size while receiving a centered `44x44dp` container;
  - booking and search icon actions now expose explicit accessibility labels.
- live verification on Android `emulator-5554`:
  - booking close: measured `44dp` minimum height;
  - each booking progress step target: measured `44x44dp`;
  - booking Back/Next CTA: larger than the minimum;
  - Home search clear: measured `44x44dp`;
  - recent-place and category chips: measured at least `44dp` high after the fix.
- automated verification:
  - shared minimum and source-usage contract;
  - Home, booking navigation, shifts, and UI primitive regressions;
  - targeted PASS: `5/5` suites, `36/36` tests;
  - full PASS: `36/36` suites, `176/176` tests, typecheck, and `git diff --check`.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-047`

### K2. Screen reader/announcements (`P1`)
- live region announcements for auth status
- semantic labels for actionable controls
- status: `verified` (2026-06-09)
- fixes:
  - Google auth state changes now use a dedicated polite/assertive live region.
  - Telegram and WhatsApp failure states expose alert semantics.
  - WhatsApp OTP input has an explicit semantic label.
  - shared loading states expose progressbar, busy, label, and polite announcement semantics.
  - empty states expose a grouped readable summary without hiding their action.
  - Cabinet tabs and booking actions use localized semantic labels and hints.

Sign-in and WhatsApp auth focus order were checked; owner Home, Business, and Cabinet tabs were then checked after authenticated login. Fixed duplicate WhatsApp validation announcements as `MB-065`; fixed Home/tab glyph and mojibake accessibility labels as `MB-066`. ## L. Performance and stability

### L1. Perceived performance (`P1`)
- first meaningful paint of key screens
- no jank during critical transitions
- status: `verified; physical release FMP and Booking transition pass` (2026-06-10)
- findings and fixes:
  - booking entry previously blocked Step 1 on business, branches, services, staff, and promotions;
  - Step 1 then launched a second business/branch query;
  - the critical path now waits only for business and branches;
  - promotions load in the background, while service/staff data remains scoped to its actual step;
  - prepared Step 1 data is passed directly to avoid the duplicate query;
  - Home passes a business preview so the booking shell immediately shows product context and progress.
- live verification:
  - uncached `Manly` booking entry showed business name, `Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р Р‹Р Р†Р вЂљРЎС™Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В°Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚в„–Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРЎв„ўР В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р РЋРЎв„ў 1 Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚в„–Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРЎв„ўР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’ВР В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРІвЂћСћР В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В· 6`, and loading status within the first live sampling window while offline;

  - corrected standalone release runs without Metro;
  - cold starts: median `401ms`, average `479.8ms`, range `335-836ms`;
  - warm resumes: median `105ms`, average `111.4ms`, range `95-142ms`;
  - cold-start frame sample: `5/35` janky frames (`14.29%`), median `6ms`, p90 `36ms`;
  - `MB-052` records and closes an ARM64 packaging crash found during this live run;
  - five authenticated warm-process Home -> Booking samples had median jank `13.89%` and p95 `21-29ms`;
  - three validated fresh-process Home -> Booking samples had median jank `17.14%`, average `16.95%`, and p95 `24-29ms`;
  - all validated transitions reached Step 1 without blank, crash, or frozen UI;
  - the initial physical result remained above budget at median `17.14%` jank.
- transition optimization and re-test (2026-06-10):
  - booking business/branch hydration now uses one atomic context update instead of three consecutive state updates;
  - promotions are deferred until native interactions complete;
  - the heavy Home -> Booking native transition animation is disabled;
  - five fresh-process samples measured `6.25%`, `7.35%`, `7.81%`, `7.35%`, and `7.25%` jank;
  - median improved to `7.35%` and average to `7.20%`, a `57.1%` relative reduction from the physical baseline;
  - all samples reached Step 1 without blank, crash, freeze, or navigation regression;
  - `MB-051` is verified and the provisional transition budget is accepted.
- build reproducibility:
  - added a mobile Metro config so release bundling resolves `apps/mobile/index.ts` correctly in the pnpm monorepo;
  - added the workspace entry bridge and explicit Babel runtime/preset dependencies so bundling does not rely on pnpm hoisting;
  - shortened pnpm virtual-store names on Windows to keep CMake object paths below the platform limit.
- automated verification:
  - post-optimization targeted PASS: `3/3` suites, `12/12` tests;
  - full PASS: `38/38` suites, `193/193` tests;
  - typecheck and `git diff --check` pass.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-050`
  - `MB-051`
  - `MB-052`

### L2. Memory and long-session stability (`P2`)
- repeated navigation loops
- no obvious memory leak symptoms
- status: `verified` (2026-06-10)
- environment:
  - standalone Android release with Hermes and embedded JS bundle;
  - Android Emulator `emulator-5554`, Pixel 7 Pro profile, `x86_64`, `1440x3120 @ 560dpi`;
  - authenticated Home session with `Low Fade` booking entry available.
- live verification:
  - completed `60` repeated loops combining Home -> Booking -> Back and Home -> Cabinet -> Home;
  - process PID remained stable (`15260`) for the entire run;
  - no `FATAL EXCEPTION`, ANR, process death, or `OutOfMemoryError` appeared in `logcat`;
  - app returned from 15 seconds in background with Home and business content intact;
  - final Android object summary remained bounded at `1` Activity, `1` ViewRoot, `5` AppContexts, and `0` WebViews.
- memory observations:
  - initial settled PSS was approximately `115.8 MB`;
  - first 30 loops warmed caches to approximately `142.1 MB` settled;
  - loops `35-60` fluctuated around `145.7-148.3 MB` under activity and returned to `140.6 MB` after settling;
  - after `trim-memory`, background, and foreground recovery, PSS remained within `136.6-139.8 MB`;
  - the second 30-loop block showed no sustained upward slope, which is consistent with cache warm-up rather than an obvious leak.
- conclusion:
  - PASS for the current P2 emulator scope;
  - no bug was logged because memory usage plateaued and remained reclaimable/stable.

### L3. Heavy-list behavior (`P2`)
- smoothness with larger lists
- pagination/virtualization behavior where applicable
- status: `verified` (2026-06-10)
- environment:
  - standalone Android release with Hermes and embedded JS bundle;
  - Android Emulator `emulator-5554`, Pixel 7 Pro profile, `x86_64`, `1440x3120 @ 560dpi`, 60Hz;
  - temporary local API fixture with `200` realistic business cards, served in pages of `20`.
- source audit:
  - Home previously rendered every returned business through `ScrollView` + `map`;
  - `/api/mobile/businesses` silently capped the result at `50` without page controls;
  - no React Native virtualized list primitive was used for the business feed.
- fix:
  - Home now uses a top-level `FlatList` so header/search/category content remains part of one virtualized scroll surface;
  - business cards are memoized and use a lighter list-card elevation;
  - rendering is bounded with `initialNumToRender=6`, `maxToRenderPerBatch=6`, `windowSize=5`, clipping, and batched updates;
  - mobile loading uses `useInfiniteQuery`, deduplicates business ids, and safely stops if an older server repeats the same unpaginated page;
  - mobile businesses API accepts bounded `page`/`limit` values and uses a Supabase range instead of a fixed first-50 cap.
- live verification:
  - release app fetched pages `1` through `10`, then page `11` returned empty and stopped pagination;
  - UIAutomator reached and displayed `Load 198`, `Load 199`, and `Load 200`;
  - no duplicate-page loop, crash, ANR, OOM, blank list, or stuck footer occurred;
  - the virtualized UI tree kept only the active window instead of all `200` cards;
  - stress PSS remained bounded (`129.0 MB` before repeated scroll, `144.2 MB` after) with `1` Activity and `0` WebViews;
  - an intentionally over-tight three-screen window reduced native Views from `429` to `181` but worsened churn, so the balanced five-screen configuration was retained.
- smoothness note:
  - aggressive ADB-generated continuous swipes on the high-resolution emulator reported `26-29%` janky frames;
  - this synthetic value is not used as a production frame budget because the same AVD was CPU/GPU-bound during command-driven input;
  - acceptance is based on bounded rendering/memory, complete pagination, no visible blank/stuck states, and no stability failures; the preferred physical-device frame gate was completed on 2026-06-19.
- supplemental emulator rerun (`LG-010`, 2026-06-19):
  - rebuilt and installed a standalone `x86_64` release on Pixel 7 Pro API 35 (`emulator-5554`, `1440x3120 @ 560dpi`);
  - a controlled local fixture served exactly `200` rows in pages of `20`; the app requested pages `1-10`, reached `Load 198-200`, requested empty page `11`, and stopped;
  - the active UIAutomator tree remained bounded at `92` nodes near the start and `104` nodes at the final rows instead of mounting all `200` cards;
  - PSS moved from `111472 KB` before the heavy scroll to `152898 KB` after all rows were accumulated, with `1` Activity and `0` WebViews;
  - Android frame timeline reported `1064` rendered frames, `9` modern janky frames (`0.85%`), and `20/23/24/29 ms` at p50/p90/p95/p99;
  - no `FATAL EXCEPTION`, ANR, `OutOfMemoryError`, duplicate-page loop, blank list, or stuck pagination state was observed;
  - an optional second shell-driven cycle was not accepted as evidence because the Windows ADB daemon stopped responding; this did not invalidate the completed first run, and the physical-device gate was subsequently closed below.
- physical-device completion (`LG-010`, 2026-06-19):
  - installed a controlled ARM64 release on Xiaomi Mi A3 (`8231be4e2ca4`), Android 11, `720x1560 @ 320dpi`, using `adb reverse` to reach the local fixture without depending on Wi-Fi;
  - Home fetched pages `1-10` with `20` rows each, displayed `Load 198-200`, requested empty page `11`, and stopped without duplicates or a stuck footer;
  - the UIAutomator tree stayed bounded at `97` nodes near the start and `95` nodes at the final rows;
  - PSS moved from `141928 KB` before the full scroll to `153935 KB` after accumulating all rows, then returned to `142448 KB` after a second 20+20 swipe cycle;
  - the first pass rendered `2832` frames with `97` janky frames (`3.43%`) and p50/p90/p95/p99 of `6/11/14/20 ms`;
  - the repeat pass rendered `2862` frames with `76` janky frames (`2.66%`) and p50/p90/p95/p99 of `6/12/14/20 ms`;
  - no crash, ANR, `OutOfMemoryError`, process death, blank list, or pagination loop was observed;
  - conclusion: PASS; the physical heavy-list performance gate is closed for the current Android baseline.
- automated verification:
  - Home screen tests cover virtualized rendering and next-page loading;
  - mobile Home suite, mobile typecheck, web pagination service/HTTP tests, web typecheck, and `git diff --check` pass.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-053`

## M. Security and privacy hygiene

### M1. Sensitive data exposure (`P0`)
- no tokens/secrets in UI logs or visible debug output
- status: `verified` (2026-06-11)
- source audit:
  - auth callback URLs were passed to development logs before masking;
  - the shared sanitizer masked sensitive object keys but did not redact tokens embedded inside arbitrary strings;
  - custom-scheme URLs did not reliably redact OAuth `code` values or fragment tokens.
- fix:
  - callback URL logging now always uses `maskUrl`;
  - the shared logger redacts bearer credentials, JWTs, auth assignments, OAuth codes, and callback tokens inside free-form strings;
  - custom-scheme URL masking now removes fragments and redacts sensitive query values;
  - production error logging continues to omit diagnostic payloads entirely.
- live verification:
  - rebuilt and installed a standalone x86_64 Android release;
  - cold-started the app through a `kezek://auth/callback` intent containing three unique sentinel secrets;
  - none of the sentinels, token parameter names, or bearer values appeared in app-process `logcat`;
  - none appeared in the UIAutomator tree or visible Sign-in screen;
  - app remained foregrounded with no debug overlay or secret-bearing error surface.
- provider live verification (`LG-009`, 2026-06-18):
  - rebuilt a clean ARM64 release APK for physical Android after the previously cached APK was missing `arm64-v8a/libexpo-modules-core.so`;
  - installed on Mi A3 `8231be4e2ca4` and completed Google account auth, native Telegram bot confirmation, and WhatsApp auth/session restoration;
  - collected app-process `logcat` and UIAutomator dumps for each provider;
  - local scans found no JWT-like values, OAuth codes, access/refresh/id tokens, callback fragments, OTPs, UUID session keys, or token-bearing key assignments;
  - no visible UI state exposed codes, tokens, or auth callback values.
- automated verification:
  - shared log safety and root navigation callback suites pass (`2/2` suites, `14/14` tests);
  - mobile and web TypeScript typechecks pass;
  - `git diff --check` passes.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-054`

### M2. Session/token storage basics (`P1`)
- secure-store usage expectations
- logout clears sensitive state
- status: `verified` (2026-06-11)
- source audit:
  - Supabase persistence uses an `expo-secure-store` adapter with chunk support for long sessions;
  - Android manifest references the Expo SecureStore backup/data-extraction exclusion rules;
  - no `AsyncStorage` session persistence is used;
  - logout previously removed the Supabase session but retained auth-flow markers, booking/shift offline data, and in-memory query data.
- fix:
  - moved session storage into a dedicated, independently tested SecureStore adapter;
  - remote sign-out now falls back to public local-scope sign-out before the legacy private removal fallback;
  - every logout clears Google, Telegram, and WhatsApp pending auth records;
  - every logout clears the active user's offline booking cache, shift queue/cache, and React Query memory cache.
- live verification:
  - standalone x86_64 release restored the authenticated session after a full emulator restart;
  - before logout, Android `SecureStore.xml` contained encrypted Supabase session chunks and a shift cache, with no plain JWT payload;
  - native Profile logout returned to Sign-in;
  - after logout, `SecureStore.xml` was empty;
  - force-stop and relaunch remained on Sign-in with no session restoration or previous-user UI.
- automated verification:
  - storage/logout core suites pass (`4/4` suites, `13/13` tests);
  - expanded auth/storage regression pack passes (`6/6` suites, `30/30` tests);
  - mobile TypeScript typecheck and `git diff --check` pass.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-055`

  - reproducible profiles created with `1080x2400 @ 480dpi` (~360dp) and `1080x2400 @ 420dpi` (~411Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРЎв„ўР В Р Р‹Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљР’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р РЋРІвЂћСћР В Р’В Р В Р вЂ№Р В Р Р‹Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›412dp).
- live coverage:
  - Home hero, search, recent locations, discovery card, category chips, and bottom tabs;
  - staff workspace cards and primary/secondary actions;
  - shift workspace header, day-off banner, status card, metrics, and disabled CTA;
  - Cabinet hero, long Telegram account label, overview metrics, sync card, and bottom navigation.
- 360dp result:
  - headings wrap without horizontal clipping;
  - search and card content stay inside the viewport;
  - workspace actions and shift metrics remain readable and tappable;
  - long account label and Cabinet statistics fit without overflow.
- 412dp result:
  - Home uses the wider one-line hero hierarchy correctly;
  - workspace and shift layouts preserve spacing and CTA width;
  - UIAutomator detected no horizontal text overflow;
  - Cabinet initially exposed an early two-column breakpoint that squeezed the Telegram account label.
- fix:
  - Cabinet hero now remains stacked through `420dp`;
  - the Profile action is full-width on 360Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В РІР‚в„ўР вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р В РІР‚В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљРЎв„ўР В Р Р‹Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р’В Р Р†Р вЂљР’В Р В Р’В Р вЂ™Р’В Р В Р вЂ Р В РІР‚С™Р РЋРІвЂћСћР В Р’В Р В Р вЂ№Р В Р Р‹Р Р†РІР‚С›РЎС›412dp mobile profiles;
  - larger widths above `420dp` retain the side-by-side layout.
- post-fix verification:
  - rebuilt and installed the standalone release;
  - repeated Cabinet visual checks at both 360dp and 412dp;
  - the long Telegram label now receives full width at 412dp without the broken `telegram` / `_...` wrap;
  - no regression appeared at 360dp.
- expanded live device matrix (`LG-011`, 2026-06-19):
  - physical Xiaomi Mi A3, Android 11, `720x1560 @ 320dpi` (real `360dp` width): authenticated Home and Cabinet rendered with zero UIAutomator bounds outside the viewport;
  - the Home hero, search, nearby card, map CTA, and bottom navigation remained readable and horizontally contained;
  - Cabinet kept the long WhatsApp account identifier readable, retained the full-width Profile action, and rendered its focus/metrics cards without horizontal clipping;
  - compact Pixel 7 Pro API 35 profile at `640x1280 @ 320dpi` (`320dp` width): the Sign-in shell displayed all three provider CTAs at once with zero viewport overflow;
  - tablet-like profile at `1280x1600 @ 320dpi` (`640dp` width): the centered Sign-in card retained controlled margins and all three CTA bounds stayed inside the viewport;
  - landscape rotation was requested while the app declares `orientation: portrait`; MainActivity stayed resumed, the complete Sign-in UI remained visible in portrait, and no blank screen, crash, ANR, or OOM occurred;
  - the emulator DNS failure that initially left Root on `Загрузка...` reproduced at default size too, so it was classified as an AVD network condition rather than a compact-layout failure; emulator app data was cleared to run the deterministic unauthenticated visual profiles;
  - conclusion: PASS for the current Android release scope; iOS remains explicitly deferred until it becomes a release target.
- automated verification:
  - Cabinet suite passes (`1/1` suite, `8/8` tests);
  - breakpoint regression covers `360`, `412`, and `421` dp;
  - mobile TypeScript typecheck passes.
- bugs found:
  - `MB-058`
